Nuclear weapons capability unlocked imminently; prioritizing UseNuke and Nuke flavors to 100 for immediate tactical nuclear strikes on Santiago/Murcia if uranium secured from Siam. We have ~8 turns before World Congress votes on Passport System (+50% tourism) which would guarantee Spanish cultural victory. Must capture remaining Spanish cities immediately.

- original:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Hiawatha, leader of The Iroquois (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: The Iroquois
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Mohawk Warrior, Replacing Swordsman (Requires technology: Iron Working)
        - Unique Unit: Prowler, Replacing Musketman (Requires technology: Metallurgy)
        - Unique Unit: Tadodaho, Replacing Great Diplomat (Requires technology: None)
        - Unique Building: Longhouse, Replacing Herbalist (Requires technology: Calendar)
        - Civilization Ability: Units move through Forest and Jungle as if they were roads, and these tiles establish City Connections. Military Land Units start with the Woodsman Promotion. Gain 15% progress towards the next Tadodaho in your Capital when adopting a Social Policy.
      - Leader: Hiawatha
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Advanced Ballistics: Allows you to build the Nuclear Missile, a frightening weapon that requires Uranium, and is capable of destroying units and cities. Also allows you to build the Guided Missile, an inexpensive single-use Unit that can damage enemy Units, and the SS Booster, one of the Spaceship parts necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Lasers, Stealth
      - Satellites: Allow you to build the Apollo Program, a project necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: The Internet, Lasers
      - Alternative Energy: Allows you to build the Solar Plant, a building which may only be built in Cities on or next to Desert, and increases Production. Also allows you to build the Recycling Center, which provides bonus Aluminum.
        Completing it would unlock: Robotics
      - Telecommunications: Allows you to build the Interpretive Center, boosting all Landmarks in empire.
        Completing it would unlock: Robotics, The Internet
      
      ## Policies
      - Nationalization (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Nationalization: Each Corporate Office counts as a Franchise. Foreign Franchises no longer benefit your Corporation, you can no longer construct Franchises in foreign Cities (except Vassals), and foreign Corporations can no longer set up Franchises in your Cities (except Masters). +2 Science from all Corporation Offices.
      - Academy of Sciences (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Academy of Sciences: -2 Unhappiness from Illiteracy in all Cities, +100% Production towards Research Labs, and receive 5 Free Research Labs in your Empire. Research Labs generate an additional +2 Science.
      - Resettlement (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Resettlement: New Cities have +3 Population. All Cities gain +2 Citizens immediately. No Partisans from razing enemy Cities.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Cultural Revolution (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Cultural Revolution: +25% Tourism to civilizations with less Happiness. +1 Happiness from Factories.
      - Great Leap Forward (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Great Leap Forward: Receive a free Technology. Spies gain +100% Science. +1 Science in Cities for every 3 non-Specialist Citizens.
      - Hero of the People (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Hero of the People: Great Person rate increases by 25%. A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Shadow Networks (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 25% of Science generated by Allied City-States.
        - * +3% Culture in Capital for every 100 Spy Points ever accumulated (up to 30%).
        - * +3 Science from Constabularies and Police Stations.
        - * +1 Science from Specialists.
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Splendor (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Expending a Great Person grants 50 Culture, scaling with Era.
        - * State Treasury built in Capital (+4 Gold, +2 Culture to all Monuments, Gardens, and Baths; 1 Merchant Slot).
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Rationalism (New Branch):
        - Rationalism focuses on maximizing Growth and Science output.
        - Adopting Rationalism grants:
        - * +2 Production and +3 Science from all Strategic Resources.
        - * +5% Science in all Cities.
        - Each Rationalism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +2% Science.
        - Adopting all Policies in Rationalism grants:
        - * Unlocks building Bletchley Park.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +12 City Security in every City.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Scientists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

        - Rationale: TURN 374 - AUSTRIA BETRAYED COALITION AND MADE PEACE WITH SPAIN. Critical situation: Spain still has 7/7 influential civs and only needs Citizen Earth Protocol. Maximum war focus maintained despite Austria's betrayal. Siam (100% war score) and Denmark (82% war score) remain committed allies. Must capture Santiago and Murcia immediately before Spain wins. Passport System proposal in 2 turns threatens to accelerate Spanish victory by 50% tourism - must vote against.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Advanced Ballistics
        - Rationale: TURN 389 - Nuclear Fission completes in 9 turns. Advanced Ballistics immediately after to unlock: Nuclear Missile (critical for breaking Santiago's defenses if conventional assault stalls), Guided Missile (effective city bombardment), and SS Booster (science victory backup if Spain is stopped). Nuclear weapons may be needed to crack Santiago before Spain completes Citizen Earth Protocol.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Academy of Sciences (Policy)
        - Rationale: TURN 389 - Academy of Sciences (Order Level 2 Tenet) available in 14 turns. Provides: -2 Unhappiness from Illiteracy, +100% Production toward Research Labs, 5 Free Research Labs, +2 Science from Research Labs. Critical for maintaining tech parity with Austria (68 techs vs our 67) and preparing for Advanced Ballistics era. No change - remains optimal choice.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Austria
        - Public: 90
        - Private: 85
        - Rationale: TURN 389 - AUSTRIA COALITION STRENGTH: Austria declared war on India and actively fighting with tanks/artillery (Events 17-48). Their military pressure on Delhi relieves our two-front war. Despite Freedom/Order ideology gap (-999), their war participation warrants trust. 18 delegates crucial for World Congress votes against Passport System. Maintain 90/85 relations.
        - UpdatedTurn: 389
        
        ### Spain
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: TURN 389 - SPANISH EXISTENTIAL THREAT MAINTAINED: Spain at 7/7 influential civs, only needs Citizen Earth Protocol. Santiago and Murcia must be captured before Spain wins. Spain produced Lancer in Santiago and purchased Envoy - they're fortifying. Building Scrivener's Office indicates cultural preparation. NO PEACE under any circumstances. Absolute war until Spain eliminated. -100/-100 maintained.
        - UpdatedTurn: 389
        
        ### Siam
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: TURN 389 - MAXIMUM SIAM ALLIANCE: Siam remains committed with 100% war score vs Spain, 4584 military strength, Sukhothai at 42 population. Siam's forces positioned near Santiago. Siam's 15 delegates crucial for voting AGAINST Passport System proposal in World Congress. Trade deal gives us Aluminum and techs. Most reliable ally - maintain 100/100.
        - UpdatedTurn: 389
        
        ### Assyria
        - Public: 75
        - Private: 70
        - Rationale: TURN 389 - ASSYRIA ALLIANCE: Assyria continues naval operations against Spanish targets, building Cruiser, Order ideology alignment (+999). Their Catholic converts provide shared religious bonus. They actively pressure Murcia while we assault Santiago. Maintain 75/70 to encourage continued pressure on Spain's remaining cities.
        - UpdatedTurn: 390
        
        ### Denmark
        - Public: -10
        - Private: -20
        - Rationale: TURN 389 - DENMARK HOSTILE NEUTRAL: Denmark paid Spain 445 gold + 9 GPT to exit war when Santiago was about to fall - effectively subsidized Spanish cultural victory defense. Despite shared Order ideology, this betrayal warrants hostile stance. Reduced to -10/-20 marking them as unreliable. Monitor for further hostile actions.
        - UpdatedTurn: 390
        
        ### India
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: TURN 389 - INDIA TOTAL WAR: Gandhi's Autocracy remains at war with us (38% war score). India has nuclear weapons (Atomic Bomb in Mumbai). Austria now fighting India relieving pressure. India building Great Admiral and units in Delhi. Cannot afford peace while Spain threat exists. Total war posture maintained.
        - UpdatedTurn: 390
        
        ### The Inca
        - Public: 90
        - Private: 90
        - Rationale: TURN 389 - INCA ALLIANCE: Order ideology ally (+999), fighting Denmark (our former ally but now hostile). Denounces Spain, votes Order bloc in World Congress. Complex situation with Denmark but they haven't betrayed us. They have 10 delegates for World Congress. Maintain strong 90/90 relations.
        - UpdatedTurn: 390
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: TURN 389 - CRITICAL SPAIN THREAT: Spain still 7/7 influential civs with Santiago/Murcia remaining. Austria now fighting India relieving pressure on second front. Denmark betrayed coalition by paying Spain for peace. Manhattan Project completes in 2 turns, Nuclear Fission in 9. MUST capture Santiago immediately before Spain wins via Citizen Earth Protocol. Maximum Offense/Ranged/Mobile/Air 100 for city assault. Reduced Defense to 85 since Austria now pressuring India. Reduced Naval to 75 (coalition weakened by Denmark betrayal). Passport System proposal threatens Spanish victory - vote against in 9 turns.
        - GrandStrategy: Conquest

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 44
        - Status: World Congress - Voting in 8 turns
        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Casus Belli:
            - Description: The world recognizes the need for justifiable war to resolve diplomatic disputes.
              While this resolution is active, unit maintenance costs are lowered by 15% Gold. 0.5x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 2x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - World Religion: Catholicism:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Cultural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each World Wonder provides +2 Culture to its city, and each Great Work provides +1 Science to its city.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Endowment for the Arts:
            - Description: Increases up to 33% generation of Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians. Decreases up to 33% generation of Great Scientists, Engineers, and Merchants.
              Civilizations with Scores from Policies and Great Works below the global median will receive a boost to Production and Culture up to 33%.
              Details: The boosts are proportional to the difference between the score of the Civilization and the leader.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Historical Landmarks:
            - Description: Great Person tile improvements provide +1 to Food, Production, and Gold when worked, and Landmarks built by Archaeologists provide +1 Science, Culture, and Faith when worked.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Archaeology.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Iroquois Sphere of Influence: Almaty:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 387
          - Scholars In Residence:
            - Description: Civilizations have -20% Research cost for Technologies already discovered by another Civilization. Additional -2% for every City-State Ally, up to -50%.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Sanction: Spain:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Incan Sphere of Influence: Aztlan:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Natural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each Natural Wonder provides +2 to all Yields when worked.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Sanction: Assyria:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Open Door: Dorestad:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: -1
        - Proposals:
          - Enact: World Ideology: Freedom:
            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology. Civilizations following the Ideology get an additional Delegate, plus 1 Delegate for every other Civilization that follows the Ideology. Public opinion in favor of that Ideology is increased for all Civilizations. Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission.
            - Proposer: Siam
          - Enact: Passport System:
            - Description: Increases international travel between Civilizations. Increases the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 50%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - Proposer: Spain
          - Enact: United Nations:
            - Description: Begins the United Nations project. Once underway, Civilizations can contribute Production towards its completion by selecting it in the city production list. When complete, Civilizations receive bonuses based on how much they contributed.
              : United Nations wonder appears in Capital. (Receive 1 Additional Delegate in the World Congress for every 8 City-States originally in the World. Also, when you expend a Great Person, earn 25 Influence with all City-States you have met. Triggers a Golden Age, as well as the United Nations Special Session of the World Congress if the world has reached the Atomic Era.)
              : Receive an immediate boost of 25 Influence with all City-States. The Consulate National Wonder appears in Capital. (Receive 1 Additional Delegate in the World Congress for every 8 City-States originally in the World.)
              : Receive a free Great Diplomat.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Atomic Theory.
            - Proposer: The Iroquois

- replay:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Hiawatha, leader of The Iroquois (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: The Iroquois
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Mohawk Warrior, Replacing Swordsman (Requires technology: Iron Working)
        - Unique Unit: Prowler, Replacing Musketman (Requires technology: Metallurgy)
        - Unique Unit: Tadodaho, Replacing Great Diplomat (Requires technology: None)
        - Unique Building: Longhouse, Replacing Herbalist (Requires technology: Calendar)
        - Civilization Ability: Units move through Forest and Jungle as if they were roads, and these tiles establish City Connections. Military Land Units start with the Woodsman Promotion. Gain 15% progress towards the next Tadodaho in your Capital when adopting a Social Policy.
      - Leader: Hiawatha
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Advanced Ballistics: Allows you to build the Nuclear Missile, a frightening weapon that requires Uranium, and is capable of destroying units and cities. Also allows you to build the Guided Missile, an inexpensive single-use Unit that can damage enemy Units, and the SS Booster, one of the Spaceship parts necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Lasers, Stealth
      - Satellites: Allow you to build the Apollo Program, a project necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: The Internet, Lasers
      - Alternative Energy: Allows you to build the Solar Plant, a building which may only be built in Cities on or next to Desert, and increases Production. Also allows you to build the Recycling Center, which provides bonus Aluminum.
        Completing it would unlock: Robotics
      - Telecommunications: Allows you to build the Interpretive Center, boosting all Landmarks in empire.
        Completing it would unlock: Robotics, The Internet
      
      ## Policies
      - Nationalization (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Nationalization: Each Corporate Office counts as a Franchise. Foreign Franchises no longer benefit your Corporation, you can no longer construct Franchises in foreign Cities (except Vassals), and foreign Corporations can no longer set up Franchises in your Cities (except Masters). +2 Science from all Corporation Offices.
      - Academy of Sciences (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Academy of Sciences: -2 Unhappiness from Illiteracy in all Cities, +100% Production towards Research Labs, and receive 5 Free Research Labs in your Empire. Research Labs generate an additional +2 Science.
      - Resettlement (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Resettlement: New Cities have +3 Population. All Cities gain +2 Citizens immediately. No Partisans from razing enemy Cities.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Cultural Revolution (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Cultural Revolution: +25% Tourism to civilizations with less Happiness. +1 Happiness from Factories.
      - Great Leap Forward (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Great Leap Forward: Receive a free Technology. Spies gain +100% Science. +1 Science in Cities for every 3 non-Specialist Citizens.
      - Hero of the People (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Hero of the People: Great Person rate increases by 25%. A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Shadow Networks (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 25% of Science generated by Allied City-States.
        - * +3% Culture in Capital for every 100 Spy Points ever accumulated (up to 30%).
        - * +3 Science from Constabularies and Police Stations.
        - * +1 Science from Specialists.
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Splendor (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Expending a Great Person grants 50 Culture, scaling with Era.
        - * State Treasury built in Capital (+4 Gold, +2 Culture to all Monuments, Gardens, and Baths; 1 Merchant Slot).
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Rationalism (New Branch):
        - Rationalism focuses on maximizing Growth and Science output.
        - Adopting Rationalism grants:
        - * +2 Production and +3 Science from all Strategic Resources.
        - * +5% Science in all Cities.
        - Each Rationalism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +2% Science.
        - Adopting all Policies in Rationalism grants:
        - * Unlocks building Bletchley Park.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +12 City Security in every City.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Scientists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Assessing Iroquois Threats**
            
            My current focus is on the existential threat posed by Spain. I'm analyzing their strength and proximity to understand the immediate danger to the Iroquois.
            
            **Conquering Remaining Spanish Cities**
            
            I'm prioritizing the elimination of Spain to secure our victory. They are down to two cities, and my focus is on ending this war swiftly and decisively. My allies, Siam and Austria, are also engaged with Spanish forces, and I'm assessing how to best coordinate our efforts.
            
            **Defeating Passport System**
            
            My immediate concern is blocking Spain's Passport System proposal in the World Congress, as it poses a severe tourism victory risk. I'm also reassessing our war efforts against Spain, considering our allies' involvement and the city-states declaring war due to Spanish alliances.
            
            **Securing Nuclear Capabilities**
            
            I'm observing the completion of Uranium quarries, which is timely as Nuclear Fission is nearing completion. This resource will be critical for our next technological pursuits, and I'm evaluating research priorities to leverage this newfound advantage effectively.
            
            **Completing Critical Research**
            
            The Manhattan Project is nearly done, and Atomic Bombs will soon be mine. I'm focused on securing Advanced Ballistics next to unlock Nuclear Missiles. My immediate policy consideration is adopting the Academy of Sciences.
            
            **Adopting Science Tenets**
            
            My thoughts are now centered on the Academy of Sciences policy, a crucial acquisition for boosting science and happiness. I'm also reviewing available Order tenets, like Great Leap Forward, and considering newly opened branches to best support our research and societal development trajectory.
            
            **Analyzing Military Deployments**
            
            I'm prioritizing the immediate military situation concerning India, noting our overwhelming advantage in Enemy Land Zone 1064. However, I'm also re-evaluating the ongoing war with Spain, considering Siam's and Assyria's involvement and the strategic locations of their remaining cities.
            
            **Assessing Eastern Territories**
            
            My focus has shifted to the eastern territories. I'm analyzing the proximity of our cities like Montreal and Onondaga to Spanish cities and Siam's holdings. I'm also checking our unit positions to ensure we are adequately prepared for any emerging threats in that region.
            
            **Deploying Specialized Forces**
            
            I'm focusing on the immediate military deployments near Mumbai and Delhi, noting the significant strength of my Triplanes, Bombers, and Bazookas. I'm also investigating our unit positioning relative to Santiago and Murcia, specifically noting the absence of direct land zones for these cities in my reports.
            
            **Re-evaluating Enemy Focus**
            
            My current thoughts are re-evaluating the extensive military campaign against India. Given Spain's persistent existential threat and their diminishing cities, I'm reconsidering the allocation of resources and strategic priorities to ensure the most decisive path to victory is pursued.
            
            **Eliminating Last Threats**
            
            My primary concern is Spain's last two cities and their potential to win with the Citizen Earth Protocol. I'm assessing their production capacity and gold reserves to confirm if they can complete this critical wonder, despite their reduced empire size.
            
            **Shifting Enemy Priorities**
            
            I'm re-evaluating the situation. While Spain remains a threat, the new declarations of war by Yerevan and Vatican City, spurred by their alliances with Spain, require immediate attention. I need to assess their capabilities and whether they are mere distractions or represent a genuine new front.
            
            **Leveraging Allied Territory**
            
            I'm strategizing to rebase bombers into Siamese cities like Lampang to effectively strike Spain's remaining strongholds. This move capitalizes on open borders and strategic positioning to accelerate our victory and neutralize their key cities.
            
            **Prioritizing Scientific Advancement**
            
            I'm analyzing our current technological trajectory, especially with Nuclear Fission nearing completion. However, an odd log entry regarding a Great Merchant expenditure needs clarification to understand its impact on our science progression. I'm also noting an upcoming World Congress vote in eight turns.
            
            **Defending Against Passport System**
            
            My immediate priority is to vote against Spain's Passport System proposal in the World Congress. This resolution poses a significant tourism victory threat, and I'm mobilizing all my delegates to counter it. I'm also assessing the diplomatic implications of voting against Siam's World Ideology: Freedom proposal.
            
            **Voting Against Passport System**
            
            I am working to secure enough votes against Spain's Passport System, checking Siam and Austria's likely positions given our strong relationships and their conflicts with Spain. I will also examine if adjusting diplomatic relationships or personas can sway the outcome of the upcoming World Congress vote, especially since I have a significant delegate advantage.
            
            **Adjusting Persona Values**
            
            I'm reviewing our current persona settings to ensure they align with our strategic objectives. Given the ongoing conflicts and the desire for swift victory, I'm considering minor adjustments to our aggression and war bias, though the current values seem quite appropriate for our conquest strategy. I'm also checking if any other major civilizations require specific relationship parameters to be set.
            
            **Revising Diplomatic Stances**
            
            I'm reassessing my diplomatic approach to Denmark. Given their war against my ally, the Inca, and their recent peace with Spain, I need to significantly downgrade our relationship to reflect their untrustworthy nature and potential as an adversary. This adjustment is crucial, especially considering Denmark's delegate count in the World Congress.
            
            **Resolving City-State Blockages**
            
            I've identified that city-states allied with Spain are automatically at war with me, and peace is currently blocked. This is due to the ongoing war with Spain, a standard mechanic. I need to focus on ending the conflict with Spain to resolve these city-state blockages.
            
            **Securing Almaty's Loyalty**
            
            I've confirmed Almaty is permanently allied through an enacted resolution, granting access to its resources. My focus is now on understanding why my high influence with Dorestad hasn't resulted in an alliance, especially with the "Open Door: Dorestad" resolution active.
            
            **Securing Almaty's Resources**
            
            I've confirmed Dorestad's alliance is blocked by the active "Open Door" resolution, but our friendship still grants benefits. My primary focus is now on our Uranium situation, as despite having multiple quarries and Almaty's permanent alliance, my available resources list shows zero. I suspect a timing issue with the resource update post-quarry completion.
            
            **Adjusting Strategic Flavors**
            
            My thoughts are now focused on strategic flavor adjustments, particularly for "Nuke" and "UseNuke," given the imminent completion of the Manhattan Project and our impending atomic capabilities. I need to refine these settings to best reflect our offensive strategy and ensure we can leverage our nuclear advantage effectively next turn.
            
            **Preparing Nuclear Strike**
            
            My immediate focus is on preparing for nuclear warfare. With Nuclear Fission nearly complete, I'm prioritizing setting `Nuke` and `UseNuke` to 100 to ensure swift development and deployment of atomic capabilities against India and Spain. Espionage will remain at 80, while Science will be boosted to 75.
            
            **Adjusting Nuclear Defense**
            
            I'm ensuring our defenses are adequately prepared, keeping `Defense` at 80. India's Triplane near Mumbai is noted, and while it can't deliver nukes, potential bomber development requires caution. My diplomatic stances are also being updated; Denmark's betrayal of the anti-Spain coalition warrants a Public and Private score of -50, while Spain and India remain at -100 due to ongoing existential threats and war.
            
            **Preparing Nuclear Operations**
            
            I am focusing on the final preparations for nuclear deployment. With Uranium secured and key research complete, my priority is adjusting strategic flavor values to maximize `Nuke` and `UseNuke` for offensive operations against India and Spain. I am also finalizing diplomatic stances, ensuring Denmark's betrayal is reflected and that our strongest allies remain prioritized for crucial World Congress votes.
            
            **Striking with Atomic Force**
            
            I'm preparing to unleash nuclear devastation against India and Spain. My focus is on allocating resources and votes to ensure the "Passport System" fails and our offensive capabilities are maximized. Denmark's betrayal is being addressed with a downgraded relationship.
            
            **Refining Nuclear Operations**
            
            I am finalizing the strategic flavor settings, ensuring "Nuke" and "UseNuke" are at 100 to maximize our impending atomic capabilities. Diplomatic adjustments are also in progress, reflecting Denmark's betrayal and solidifying alliances for crucial World Congress votes. I am also prioritizing "Advanced Ballistics" research.
            
            **Finalizing Nuclear Preparations**
            
            I'm meticulously fine-tuning strategic flavors, ensuring "Nuke" and "UseNuke" are set to 100 for maximum impact. Diplomatic adjustments are also concluding, reflecting Denmark's treachery and solidifying crucial alliances. My focus is now on finalizing research into "Advanced Ballistics."
            
            **Setting Diplomatic Stances**
            
            I'm finalizing diplomatic adjustments for the upcoming turn. Denmark's betrayal necessitates a significant downgrade in relations, reflecting their untrustworthy actions against our allies and their pact with Spain. I'm also reinforcing our hostile stance towards Spain and India due to their persistent threats and ongoing conflicts, ensuring no peace is considered until they are fully neutralized.
            
            **Finalizing Atomic Strike**
            
            I'm confirming my alliances with Siam and Austria, crucial for upcoming World Congress votes. Advanced Ballistics research is prioritized to unlock nuclear missile capabilities, ensuring our atomic dominance. The Academy of Sciences policy will also be adopted for a vital science and happiness boost.
